Start exploring now!<br>Kimi API Platform home page<br>Guides<br>API Reference<br>Models &amp; Pricing<br>Resources<br>Search...<br>⌘ K<br>Kimi Business<br>Contact Sales<br>User Center<br>Kimi API Platform home page Search or ask...<br>Navigation<br>Model Pricing<br>Model Inference Pricing<br>Model Pricing<br>Overview<br>Other Pricing<br>BatchJob Pricing<br>WebSearch Pricing<br>Top-up &amp; Rate Limits<br>Recharge and Rate Limits<br>Models<br>Kimi K3.1 Pro<br>Kimi K3<br>Kimi K2.7 Code<br>Kimi K2.6<br>Model List<br>Model Pricing<br>Model Inference Pricing<br>Copy page Copy page<br>Understand token billing, input and output charges, cache discounts, and pricing links for Kimi model inference.<br>Copy page Copy page<br>​<br>Model Pricing<br>​<br>K3 Series Models<br>All K3 series models support a 1,048,576-token context window.<br>Note: The Kimi API automatically enables context caching billing for repeated request prefixes, with a default TTL (time-to-live) of 5m. For TTL settings and cost optimization tips, see Best Practices for Context Caching .<br>Batch API Inference : kimi-k3.1-pro and kimi-k3 support batch inference for large-scale tasks that do not require real-time responses, billed at 60% of the standard price.<br>​<br>K2 Series Models<br>All K2 series models support a 262,144-token context window.<br>Dear Kimi Users, starting October 31, 2026, K2 series models will no longer be available to newly registered users; existing users are not affected. We plan to retire all K2 series models by the end of December 2026, please prepare your migration in advance.<br>1 MTok = 1,000,000 tokens. All prices above are per MTok consumed.<br>Explanation: Prices exclude applicable taxes. Specific tax obligations are subject to local tax regulations and will be calculated at checkout based on your jurisdiction.<br>​<br>Concepts<br>Model inference on the Kimi API platform is billed on a pay-as-you-go basis, with no subscription plan. It differs from products such as the Kimi membership and Kimi Code. See the product comparison .<br>​<br>Billing Unit<br>Token: the basic unit of text processed by the model. A long word like “antidisestablishmentarianism” may be split into several tokens, while a common short word like “word” usually takes just one. For typical English text, 1 token is roughly equivalent to 3-4 characters. Use the Token Calculation API to get the exact token count of each call.<br>​<br>Billing Logic<br>Model inference APIs bill input and output tokens by usage. For K3 series models, cache writes are billed separately by TTL (5min / 1h); cached input is billed at the cache-hit price only, with no additional cache write charge. If you upload a document, extract its content, and pass the extracted content as model input, that content is also billed by usage. File-related APIs (file content extraction and file storage) are temporarily free : uploading and extracting a document alone incurs no file API charges. For tips on improving cache hit rates and choosing a TTL tier, see Best Practices for Context Caching .<br>​<br>Model Notes<br>See each model’s guide for capabilities and recommended use cases:<br>Kimi K3.1 Pro<br>Kimi K3<br>Kimi K2.7 Code<br>Kimi K2.6<br>Was this page helpful?<br>Yes<br>No<br>BatchJob Pricing<br>Next<br>⌘ I<br>x github linkedin discord website<br>On this page<br>Back to top Model Pricing K3 Series Models<br>K2 Series Models<br>Concepts Billing Unit<br>Billing Logic<br>Model Notes | Model Inference Pricing - Kimi API Platform<br>Documentation Index<br>Fetch the complete documentation index at: /docs/llms.txt<br>Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br>Skip to main content<br>🎉 Kimi K3.1 Pro has launched! | excerpt | 更新 | WebSearch Pricing - Kimi API Platform<br>Documentation Index<br>Fetch the complete documentation index at: /docs/llms.txt<br>Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br>Skip to main content<br>🎉 Kimi K3.1 Pro has launched! Start exploring now!<br>Kimi API Platform home page<br>Guides<br>API Reference<br>Models &amp; Pricing<br>Resources<br>Search...<br>⌘ K<br>Kimi Business<br>Contact Sales<br>User Center<br>Kimi API Platform home page Search or ask...<br>Navigation<br>Other Pricing<br>WebSearch Pricing<br>Model Pricing<br>Overview<br>Other Pricing<br>BatchJob Pricing<br>WebSearch Pricing<br>Top-up &amp; Rate Limits<br>Recharge and Rate Limits<br>Models<br>Kimi K3.1 Pro<br>Kimi K3<br>Kimi K2.7 Code<br>Kimi K2.6<br>Model List<br>Other Pricing<br>WebSearch Pricing<br>Copy page Copy page<br>Review pricing, billing units, and usage notes for the Kimi web-search and fetch tools.<br>Copy page Copy page<br>​<br>Product Pricing<br>Explanation: Prices exclude applicable taxes. Specific tax obligations are subject to local tax regulations and will be calculated at checkout based on your jurisdiction.<br>Web search and fetch capabilities are billed per call; each channel is independent and billed separately:<br>Failed requests, searches not triggered by the model, or empty results/content are not billed. When using web search via the Responses API or the Formula API, model input/output Tokens are still billed at the corresponding model pricing .<br>​<br>Billing Details<br>​<br>Web Search Basic / Web Search Pro<br>Calls to /v1/tools/search or /v1/tools/search_pro :<br>One call charge applies when the request succeeds (HTTP 200) and the search_results field in the response is non-empty (at least one search result is returned).<br>Requests that fail (invalid parameters, rate limiting, server errors, timeouts, etc.) or return no search results are not billed.<br>Web Search Pro additionally supports restricting results to specific sites via sites , constraining the result time range via time_window , and returns structured content snippets ( chunks ) for each result.<br>For endpoint parameters and usage examples, see Best Practices for Web Search .<br>​<br>URL Fetch<br>Calls to /v1/tools/fetch :<br>One call charge applies when the request succeeds (HTTP 200) and the markdown field in the response is non-blank (page content was successfully extracted).<br>Requests that fail (invalid URL, security risk control, rate limiting, server errors, timeouts, etc.), pages with no extractable content (404 markdown_not_found ), or empty fetched content are not billed.<br>For endpoint parameters and usage examples, see Best Practices for Web Search .<br>​<br>Responses API web_search<br>Add {"type": "web_search"} to the tools array of POST /v1/responses :<br>The server evaluates the input to determine whether a search is required; if so, it executes the search and injects the results into the model context.<br>One call charge applies when the server executes a search (a web_search_call item is present in output ); no charge applies if the model does not trigger a search.<br>Including image in search_content_types (image search) does not incur an additional charge.<br>See Responses API .<br>​<br>Formula API web-search<br>On kimi-k3 , each execution of the official web-search tool through the Formula API ( POST /v1/formulas/{uri}/fibers ) incurs one call charge. See How to Use Official Tools in Kimi API .<br>Was this page helpful?<br>Yes<br>No<br>BatchJob Pricing<br>Previous<br>Recharge and Rate Limiting<br>Next<br>⌘ I<br>x github linkedin discord website<br>On this page<br>Back to top Product Pricing<br>Billing Details Web Search Basic / Web Search Pro<br>URL Fetch<br>Responses API web_search<br>Formula API web-search | WebSearch Pricing - Kimi API Platform<br>Documentation Index<br>Fetch the complete documentation index at: /docs/llms.txt<br>Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.<br>Skip to main content<br>🎉 Kimi K3.1 Pro has launched! ## moonshotai 专属证据与核对说明

### Kimi 官方资料 IR 观察候选

- IR 指纹：`sha256:8829f7e59b4fb3735a6df76285b81a89c51d2d8e6a638ad2a86edf50e93c138d`
- 来源快照：28 份
- 中国目录 ID：21 个
- 国际目录 ID：21 个
- 两市场当前目录已投影为 Offering；模型级 Chat Completions、Responses 与 Anthropic Messages Route 按官方支持范围映射。Batch 支持型号、创建 Route、请求参数、异步输出与 60% 价格已映射。联网搜索和网页抓取已映射为独立 Capability；普通模型请求参数与输入输出 Schema、工具响应语义/错误边界及会员计划仍需 Review。

#### 待人工核对

- search_results 语义：搜索结果列表；无结果时为空数组。；响应必填字段：search_results；ErrorResponse 必填字段：error；文档错误 HTTP 状态码：400, 401, 403, 408, 429, 500, 502, 504。 当前公共 CapabilityOutput 仅能保存响应字段名，不能表达必填字段、空结果约束或错误响应状态/结构；这些响应语义保留 Review，不视为已完整映射。
- 响应必填字段：url, markdown, title；ErrorResponse 必填字段：error；文档错误 HTTP 状态码：400, 401, 403, 404, 408, 429, 500, 502, 504。 当前公共 CapabilityOutput 仅能保存响应字段名，不能表达必填字段、空结果约束或错误响应状态/结构；这些响应语义保留 Review，不视为已完整映射。
- search_results 语义：List of search results; an empty array when nothing matches.；响应必填字段：search_results；ErrorResponse 必填字段：error；文档错误 HTTP 状态码：400, 401, 403, 408, 429, 500, 502, 504。 当前公共 CapabilityOutput 仅能保存响应字段名，不能表达必填字段、空结果约束或错误响应状态/结构；这些响应语义保留 Review，不视为已完整映射。
- 响应必填字段：url, markdown, title；ErrorResponse 必填字段：error；文档错误 HTTP 状态码：400, 401, 403, 404, 408, 429, 500, 502, 504。 当前公共 CapabilityOutput 仅能保存响应字段名，不能表达必填字段、空结果约束或错误响应状态/结构；这些响应语义保留 Review，不视为已完整映射。
- 当前目录已按市场映射为 listed_current Offering；协议文档中明确声明的模型级 Route 已按协议分别生成 Operation，直接类型请求参数、messages/input 输入项和结构化响应外层字段已提取。oneOf 联合类型、复杂嵌套消息/媒体结构、完整输出约束、工具响应必需性及错误边界仍在 Review。
- Batch 按官方支持型号、异步创建 API 和标准模型价格 60% 映射为独立批处理 Operation 定价；会员订阅计划仍需与 API 按量价格分别核对。
- 官方 Token 价格行「kimi-k2.7-code」未能可靠归一；保留在来源观察中，不生成 PriceFact。
